000101 |     else
000102 |         echo "生态可达性=fail:claude.ai:$r1 api:$r2"
000103 |     fi
000104 | }
000105 | 
000106 | fix() {
000107 |     echo "FIX:开始一键修复"
000108 | 
000109 |     if ! core_up; then
000110 |         echo "FIX:正在尝试启动 Clash Verge..."
000111 |         (clash-verge >/dev/null 2>&1 &) || (clash-verge-rev >/dev/null 2>&1 &) || echo "FIX:未找到启动命令,请手动启动 Clash Verge"
000112 |     fi
000113 |     for i in $(seq 1 40); do
000114 |         core_up && tun_up && break
000115 |         sleep 1
000116 |     done
000117 |     if core_up && tun_up; then
000118 |         echo "FIX:代理内核+TUN ✓"
000119 |     else
000120 |         echo "FIX:代理内核/TUN ✗(请检查 Clash Verge 的 TUN 开关与节点)"
000121 |     fi

000128 |     if [ -n "$adminCmds" ]; then
000129 |         echo "FIX:修复时区/IPv6(需要 sudo 密码)..."
000130 |         sudo bash -c "$adminCmds" && echo "FIX:时区/IPv6 ✓" || echo "FIX:时区/IPv6 ✗"
000131 |     else
000132 |         echo "FIX:时区/IPv6 无需修复 ✓"
000133 |     fi
000134 |     command -v resolvectl >/dev/null 2>&1 && resolvectl flush-caches 2>/dev/null
000135 |     command -v systemd-resolve >/dev/null 2>&1 && systemd-resolve --flush-caches 2>/dev/null
000136 | 
000137 |     # 保镖(systemd user 服务,免 sudo)
000138 |     if ! systemctl --user is-active claude-bodyguard.service >/dev/null 2>&1; then
000139 |         echo "FIX:注册并启动保镖服务..."
000140 |         mkdir -p "$HOME/.config/systemd/user"
000141 |         cat > "$HOME/.config/systemd/user/claude-bodyguard.service" <<EOF

000152 | EOF

000190 | status() {
000191 |     cur="none"; [ -f "$BASE/current" ] && cur=$(cat "$BASE/current")
000192 |     hasA=0; [ -d "$BASE/accounts/A" ] && hasA=1
000193 |     hasB=0; [ -d "$BASE/accounts/B" ] && hasB=1
000194 |     echo "current=$cur hasA=$hasA hasB=$hasB"
000195 | }
000196 | 
000197 | quit_browser() {
000198 |     pgrep -f 'chrome|chromium' >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
000199 |     pkill -f "$DATA" 2>/dev/null
000200 |     for i in $(seq 1 15); do
000201 |         pgrep -f "$DATA" >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
000202 |         sleep 1
000203 |     done
000204 |     return 1
000205 | }
000206 | 
000207 | archive_current() {
000208 |     cur="$1"
000209 |     rm -rf "$BASE/accounts/$cur.tmp"
000210 |     cp -a "$DATA" "$BASE/accounts/$cur.tmp" && rm -rf "$BASE/accounts/$cur" && mv "$BASE/accounts/$cur.tmp" "$BASE/accounts/$cur"
000211 |     log "已存档账号$cur(浏览器登录态)"
000212 | }
000213 | 
000214 | switch_to() {
000215 |     target="$1"
000216 |     cur="none"; [ -f "$BASE/current" ] && cur=$(cat "$BASE/current")
000217 | 
000218 |     if [ "$cur" = "none" ] && [ -d "$DATA" ] && [ -n "$(ls -A "$DATA" 2>/dev/null)" ]; then
000219 |         quit_browser || { echo "FAIL:浏览器未能退出,请手动关闭后重试"; return 1; }
000220 |         archive_current A
000221 |         cur="A"; echo "$cur" > "$BASE/current"
000222 |         log "首次运行:当前浏览器登录态已收编为账号A"
000223 |         echo "NOTE:已将当前登录收编为账号A"
000224 |     fi
000225 | 
000226 |     if [ "$cur" = "$target" ]; then
000227 |         echo "INFO:当前已是账号$target"
000228 |         return 0
000229 |     fi
000230 | 
000231 |     quit_browser || { echo "FAIL:浏览器未能退出,请手动关闭后重试"; return 1; }
000232 |     [ "$cur" != "none" ] && [ -d "$DATA" ] && archive_current "$cur"
000233 | 
000234 |     rm -rf "$DATA.new"
000235 |     if [ -d "$BASE/accounts/$target" ]; then
000236 |         cp -a "$BASE/accounts/$target" "$DATA.new"
000237 |         log "已载入账号$target 存档"
000238 |     else
000239 |         mkdir -p "$DATA.new"
000240 |         log "账号$target 无存档,将以全新状态初始化"
000241 |     fi
000242 |     rm -rf "$DATA" && mv "$DATA.new" "$DATA"
000243 |     echo "$target" > "$BASE/current"
000244 |     log "已切换到账号$target"
000245 |     echo "OK:已切换到账号$target(请重新打开浏览器)"
000246 | }
000247 | 
000248 | menu() {
000249 |     while true; do
000250 |         clear
000251 |         echo "=============================="
000252 |         echo "  Claude 账号切换器 (Linux)"
000253 |         echo "=============================="
000254 |         echo ""
000255 |         echo "  1) 安全检查 (checkdetail)"
000256 |         echo "  2) 一键修复 (fix)"
000257 |         echo "  3) 切换到账号 A"
000258 |         echo "  4) 切换到账号 B"
000259 |         echo "  5) 查看状态 (status)"
000260 |         echo "  0) 退出"
000261 |         echo ""
000262 |         read -r -p "选择: " c
000263 |         case "$c" in
000264 |             1) checkdetail; read -r -p "回车继续..." _ ;;
000265 |             2) fix; read -r -p "回车继续..." _ ;;
000266 |             3) switch_to A; read -r -p "回车继续..." _ ;;
000267 |             4) switch_to B; read -r -p "回车继续..." _ ;;
000268 |             5) status; read -r -p "回车继续..." _ ;;
000269 |             0) exit 0 ;;
000270 |         esac
000271 |     done
000272 | }
000273 | 
000274 | case "${1:-menu}" in
000275 |     checkdetail) OUT="$BASE/last-check.txt"; { checkdetail; } > "$OUT" 2>&1; cat "$OUT" ;;
000276 |     status) status ;;
000277 |     fix) fix ;;
000278 |     switch) switch_to "${2:-}" ;;
000279 |     menu|*) menu ;;
000280 | esac

FILE: original_HEAD/linux/stun-probe.py
SHA256: 2c891f5f3cf392a6bb736dacc65a945afe89ae8e4d88e353b04fd1f9cd792ab1
==============================================================================
000001 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
000002 | """WebRTC 泄漏探测:并发向多个 STUN 服务器(含国内节点)发 UDP Binding 请求。
000003 | 走隧道时应得到代理出口 IP,裸连时会暴露真实国内 IP。全程硬超时 10 秒,绝不阻塞。"""
000004 | import socket, struct, os, signal, threading
000005 | 
000006 | SERVERS = [
000007 |     ("stun.miwifi.com", 3478),        # 国内节点:裸连时必有响应
000008 |     ("stun.chat.bilibili.com", 3478), # 国内节点
000009 |     ("stun.l.google.com", 19302),     # 全球节点
000010 |     ("stun.cloudflare.com", 3478),    # 全球节点
000011 | ]
000012 | 
000013 | def stun_public_ip(host, port, timeout=2.0):
000014 |     try:
000015 |         addr = socket.gethostbyname(host)
000016 |     except Exception:
000017 |         return None
000018 |     txn = os.urandom(12)
000019 |     req = struct.pack("!HHI", 0x0001, 0, 0x2112A442) + txn
000020 |     s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
000021 |     s.settimeout(timeout)
000022 |     try:
000023 |         s.sendto(req, (addr, port))
000024 |         data, _ = s.recvfrom(1024)
000025 |     except Exception:
000026 |         return None
000027 |     finally:
000028 |         s.close()
000029 |     if len(data) < 20 or data[0:2] != b"\x01\x01":
000030 |         return None
000031 |     pos = 20
000032 |     while pos + 4 <= len(data):
000033 |         atype, alen = struct.unpack("!HH", data[pos:pos+4])
000034 |         aval = data[pos+4:pos+4+alen]
000035 |         if atype == 0x0020 and len(aval) >= 8 and aval[1] == 0x01:
000036 |             ip_int = struct.unpack("!I", aval[4:8])[0] ^ 0x2112A442
000037 |             return socket.inet_ntoa(struct.pack("!I", ip_int))
000038 |         if atype == 0x0001 and len(aval) >= 8 and aval[1] == 0x01:
000039 |             return socket.inet_ntoa(aval[4:8])
000040 |         pos += 4 + alen + ((4 - alen % 4) % 4)
000041 |     return None
000042 | 
000043 | def _alarm(signum, frame):
000044 |     raise TimeoutError
000045 | 
000046 | def main():
000047 |     found = []
000048 |     lock = threading.Lock()
000049 |     def worker(h, p):
000050 |         ip = stun_public_ip(h, p)
000051 |         if ip:
000052 |             with lock:
000053 |                 if ip not in found:
000054 |                     found.append(ip)
000055 |     threads = []
000056 |     for h, p in SERVERS:
000057 |         t = threading.Thread(target=worker, args=(h, p), daemon=True)
000058 |         t.start()
000059 |         threads.append(t)
000060 |     for t in threads:
000061 |         t.join(timeout=3)
000062 |     print("RESULT:" + (",".join(found) if found else "NONE"))
000063 | 
000064 | signal.signal(signal.SIGALRM, _alarm)
000065 | signal.alarm(10)
000066 | try:
000067 |     main()
000068 | except TimeoutError:
000069 |     print("RESULT:NONE")

000001 | #!/bin/bash
000002 | # 重新生成仓库根 SHA256SUMS
000003 | # 维护纪律: core/、daemons/、installer/ 下任何文件改动后,提交前必须运行本脚本
000004 | cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." || exit 1

000006 |     core/claude-switch.sh \
000007 |     daemons/claude-bodyguard.sh \

000009 |     daemons/local.claude-bodyguard.plist \

000011 |     daemons/local.claude-update.plist \
000012 |     installer/安装.command \
000013 |     installer/卸载.command > SHA256SUMS
000014 | echo "✓ SHA256SUMS 已更新"

FILE: original_HEAD/windows/README.md
SHA256: 390c9dd2371754be2c3bef29b5bf46c7aaffa7f0ba886bc9f41052fedae92220
==============================================================================
000001 | # Claude 切换器 · Windows 版 (Beta)
000002 | 
000003 | > ⚠️ **本版本为 Beta,未经大规模真机测试**。核心逻辑移植自 macOS 稳定版,但 Windows 环境差异大,遇到问题请到 [Issues](https://github.com/YOURSJEYO/claude-switcher/issues) 反馈。
000004 | 
000005 | ## 系统要求
000006 | 
000007 | - Windows 10 / 11 (64 位)
000008 | - PowerShell 5.1+（Windows 自带,无需安装）
000009 | - [Clash Verge](https://github.com/clash-verge-rev/clash-verge-rev) Windows 版,且 **TUN 模式可用**
000010 | - Claude 桌面版 Windows
000011 | - 安装时需要**管理员权限**一次（注册守护任务 / 锁定时区 / 关闭 IPv6）
000012 | 
000013 | ## 安装
000014 | 
000015 | 1. 先把 Clash Verge 开好（TUN 模式 + 美国节点）——安装器会校验出口必须是美国
000016 | 2. 右键 `install.ps1` →「使用 PowerShell 运行」,或在管理员 PowerShell 中执行
000017 | 3. 如果提示禁止运行脚本,先执行一次:
000018 |    ```powershell
000019 |    Set-ExecutionPolicy -Scope CurrentUser RemoteSigned
000020 |    ```
000021 | 4. 按提示走完,桌面会出现「Claude切换器」快捷方式
000022 | 
000023 | ## 与 macOS 版的差异（注意事项）
000024 | 
000025 | > **注意**：macOS 版已支持多代理客户端（Clash Verge / Shadowrocket / sing-box / ClashX / Surge）与地区自适应（按出口国家自动锁定时区+地区格式，换节点后 `set-region` 一键重配）。Windows 版当前仍默认按美国出口设计，安装时会按 ipinfo 自动锁定时区；其他国家/地区的完整支持在路线图中。另外，自动更新（`update` 命令 + 12 小时自动检查）与 48 小时 IP 审查目前仅 macOS 版提供，后续版本同步。
000026 | 
000027 | | 项目 | 说明 |
000028 | |---|---|
000029 | | 时区名称 | Windows 用 `Eastern Standard Time` 这类 ID,安装器会自动把 IP 城市映射过去 |
000030 | | 保镖频率 | 计划任务登录启动,隐藏窗口常驻,每 10 秒巡检(与 Mac 一致) |
000031 | | IPv6 | 关闭的是所有网卡的 ms_tcpip6 绑定;要恢复: `Enable-NetAdapterBinding -ComponentID ms_tcpip6` |
000032 | | SmartScreen | 首次运行可能弹"Windows 已保护你的电脑" → 点「更多信息」→「仍要运行」 |
000033 | | 账号数据目录 | Windows 版在 `%APPDATA%\Claude`(与 macOS 路径不同,逻辑相同) |
000034 | | 卸载 | 计划任务里删掉 ClaudeBodyguard,删除 `%USERPROFILE%\.claude-switcher` 和桌面快捷方式即可 |
000035 | 
000036 | ## 已知限制
000037 | 
000038 | - 界面是 WPF 重写的第一版,样式以 Mac 版为准对齐,细节可能有出入
000039 | - Clash Verge 的 TUN 开关在 Windows 上如果被更新重置,目前需要手动在设置里重新打开(一键修复会提示)
000040 | - 账号快照切换前请确保 Claude 已完全退出(托盘图标也要退出)

000099 | function Invoke-Core([string[]]$coreArgs) {
000100 |     $out = & powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File $CORE @coreArgs 2>$null
000101 |     return ($out | Out-String).Trim()
000102 | }

000104 | function Refresh-Checks {
000105 |     $rows.Clear()
000106 |     foreach ($n in $checkNames) { $rows.Add([pscustomobject]@{ Name = $n; Detail = ''; Icon = '…'; Color = '#FF6B7280' }) }
000107 |     Set-Status '检查中' ([System.Windows.Media.Color]::FromRgb(0xFB,0x92,0x3C))
000108 |     $win.FindName('Banner').Text = ''
000109 | 
000110 |     $out = Invoke-Core @('checkdetail')
000111 |     $allOk = $true; $matched = 0
000112 |     foreach ($line in ($out -split "`n")) {
000113 |         $kv = $line -split '=', 2
000114 |         if ($kv.Count -ne 2) { continue }
000115 |         $st = $kv[1] -split ':', 2
000116 |         for ($i = 0; $i -lt $rows.Count; $i++) {
000117 |             if ($rows[$i].Name -eq $kv[0] -or ($kv[0] -eq 'TUN网卡' -and $rows[$i].Name -eq 'TUN网卡')) {
000118 |                 $matched++
000119 |                 $d = if ($st.Count -gt 1) { $st[1] } else { '' }
000120 |                 switch ($st[0]) {
000121 |                     'ok'   { $rows[$i] = [pscustomobject]@{ Name = $rows[$i].Name; Detail = $d; Icon = '✓'; Color = '#FF22C55E' } }
000122 |                     'warn' { $rows[$i] = [pscustomobject]@{ Name = $rows[$i].Name; Detail = $d; Icon = '⚠'; Color = '#FFFACC15' } }
000123 |                     default { $rows[$i] = [pscustomobject]@{ Name = $rows[$i].Name; Detail = $d; Icon = '✗'; Color = '#FFF87171' }; $allOk = $false }
000124 |                 }
000125 |                 break
000126 |             }
000127 |         }
000128 |     }
000129 |     $st = Invoke-Core @('status')
000130 |     $win.FindName('CurrentAccount').Text = if ($st -match 'current=B') { 'B' } elseif ($st -match 'current=A') { 'A' } else { '未初始化' }
000131 |     if ($matched -lt $checkNames.Count) {
000132 |         Set-Status '检查未过' ([System.Windows.Media.Color]::FromRgb(0xF8,0x71,0x71))
000133 |         $win.FindName('Banner').Text = "检测输出不完整($matched/$($checkNames.Count))，请点重新检查"

000135 |         Set-Status '环境安全' ([System.Windows.Media.Color]::FromRgb(0x22,0xC5,0x5E))
000136 |     } else {
000137 |         Set-Status '检查未过' ([System.Windows.Media.Color]::FromRgb(0xF8,0x71,0x71))
000138 |         $win.FindName('Banner').Text = '存在未通过项，可一键修复或手动处理后重试'
000139 |     }
000140 | }

000001 | # Claude 保镖 (Windows) —— 代理离线时自动关闭 Claude
000002 | # 由安装器注册为计划任务,登录时启动,隐藏窗口常驻
000003 | $ErrorActionPreference = 'SilentlyContinue'
000004 | $LOG = Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE ".claude-switcher\bodyguard.log"
000005 | 
000006 | while ($true) {
000007 |     $coreUp = [bool](Get-Process -Name 'verge-mihomo' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
000008 |     $tunUp  = [bool](Get-NetIPAddress -AddressFamily IPv4 | Where-Object { $_.IPAddress -like '198.18.*' })
000009 |     if (-not ($coreUp -and $tunUp)) {
000010 |         $p = Get-Process -Name 'Claude' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
000011 |         if ($p) {
000012 |             $p | Stop-Process -Force
000013 |             "$(Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss') 代理离线(内核或TUN丢失),已自动关闭 Claude" | Out-File -Append -Encoding utf8 $LOG
000014 |         }
000015 |     }
000016 |     Start-Sleep -Seconds 10
000017 | }

000022 | function Get-Egress {
000023 |     $trace = & curl.exe -s --max-time 8 "https://www.cloudflare.com/cdn-cgi/trace" 2>$null
000024 |     $ip  = (($trace -split "`n" | Where-Object { $_ -like 'ip=*' })  -replace 'ip=','').Trim()
000025 |     $loc = (($trace -split "`n" | Where-Object { $_ -like 'loc=*' }) -replace 'loc=','').Trim()
000026 |     return @{ ip = $ip; loc = $loc }
000027 | }
000028 | 
000029 | # STUN 探针(纯 .NET UDP,手工构造 RFC5389 报文)
000030 | function Get-StunPublicIP([string]$server, [int]$port, [int]$timeoutMs = 2000) {
000031 |     try { $addr = ([System.Net.Dns]::GetHostAddresses($server) | Where-Object { $_.AddressFamily -eq 'InterNetwork' } | Select-Object -First 1) } catch { return $null }
000032 |     if (-not $addr) { return $null }
000033 |     $udp = New-Object System.Net.Sockets.UdpClient
000034 |     $udp.Client.ReceiveTimeout = $timeoutMs
000035 |     try {
000036 |         $txn = New-Object byte[] 12; (New-Object Random).NextBytes($txn)
000037 |         $req = [byte[]](0x00, 0x01, 0x00, 0x00) + [byte[]](0x21, 0x12, 0xA4, 0x42) + $txn
000038 |         $udp.Connect($addr, $port)
000039 |         [void]$udp.Send($req, $req.Length)
000040 |         $remote = New-Object System.Net.IPEndPoint([System.Net.IPAddress]::Any, 0)
000041 |         $resp = $udp.Receive([ref]$remote)
000042 |         $pos = 20
000043 |         while ($pos + 4 -le $resp.Length) {
000044 |             $atype = ([int]$resp[$pos] -shl 8) -bor $resp[$pos + 1]
000045 |             $alen  = ([int]$resp[$pos + 2] -shl 8) -bor $resp[$pos + 3]
000046 |             if (($atype -eq 0x20 -or $atype -eq 0x01) -and $alen -ge 8 -and $resp[$pos + 5] -eq 0x01) {
000047 |                 $ipBytes = @($resp[$pos + 8], $resp[$pos + 9], $resp[$pos + 10], $resp[$pos + 11])
000048 |                 if ($atype -eq 0x20) {
000049 |                     $cookie = [byte[]](0x21, 0x12, 0xA4, 0x42)
000050 |                     for ($i = 0; $i -lt 4; $i++) { $ipBytes[$i] = $ipBytes[$i] -bxor $cookie[$i] }
000051 |                 }
000052 |                 return ($ipBytes -join '.')
000053 |             }
000054 |             $pos += 4 + $alen + ((4 - ($alen % 4)) % 4)
000055 |         }
000056 |     } catch { return $null } finally { $udp.Close() }
000057 |     return $null
000058 | }

000060 | function Test-WebRTC($exitIp) {
000061 |     $servers = @(@("stun.miwifi.com", 3478), @("stun.chat.bilibili.com", 3478), @("stun.l.google.com", 19302), @("stun.cloudflare.com", 3478))
000062 |     $found = @()
000063 |     foreach ($s in $servers) {
000064 |         $r = Get-StunPublicIP $s[0] $s[1]
000065 |         if ($r -and ($found -notcontains $r)) { $found += $r }
000066 |     }
000067 |     if ($found.Count -eq 0) { return @{ code = 'ok'; detail = 'UDP不出网,零暴露' } }
000068 |     $leak = $found | Where-Object { $_ -ne $exitIp }
000069 |     if ($leak) { return @{ code = 'fail'; detail = "暴露非代理IP: $($leak -join ',')" } }
000070 |     return @{ code = 'ok'; detail = '仅暴露代理IP' }
000071 | }

000210 | function Save-Account($name) {
000211 |     robocopy "$DATA" "$BASE\accounts\$name" /MIR /NFL /NDL /NJH /NJS | Out-Null
000212 |     Log "已存档账号$name"
000213 | }
000214 | 
000215 | function Start-ClaudeApp {
000216 |     $exe = @("$env:LOCALAPPDATA\AnthropicClaude\Claude.exe", "$env:LOCALAPPDATA\Programs\Claude\Claude.exe") | Where-Object { Test-Path $_ } | Select-Object -First 1
000217 |     if ($exe) { Start-Process $exe } else { Start-Process "shell:AppsFolder" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
000218 | }
000219 | 
000220 | function Invoke-Switch($target) {
000221 |     $cur = if (Test-Path "$BASE\current.txt") { (Get-Content "$BASE\current.txt" -Raw).Trim() } else { 'none' }
000222 | 
000223 |     # 首次运行:收编当前登录为账号A
000224 |     if ($cur -eq 'none' -and (Test-Path $DATA) -and (Get-ChildItem $DATA -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
000225 |         if (-not (Stop-ClaudeApp)) { "FAIL:Claude 未能退出，请手动退出后重试"; return }
000226 |         Save-Account A
000227 |         $cur = 'A'; 'A' | Out-File -Encoding ascii -NoNewline "$BASE\current.txt"
000228 |         Log "首次运行:当前登录状态已收编为账号A"
000229 |         "NOTE:已将当前登录收编为账号A"
000230 |     }
000231 | 
000232 |     if ($cur -eq $target) { Start-ClaudeApp; "INFO:当前已是账号$target"; return }
000233 | 
000234 |     if (-not (Stop-ClaudeApp)) { "FAIL:Claude 未能退出，请手动退出后重试"; return }
000235 | 
000236 |     if ($cur -ne 'none' -and (Test-Path $DATA)) { Save-Account $cur }
000237 | 
000238 |     if (Test-Path "$BASE\accounts\$target") {
000239 |         Remove-Item $DATA -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
000240 |         robocopy "$BASE\accounts\$target" "$DATA" /E /NFL /NDL /NJH /NJS | Out-Null
000241 |         Log "已载入账号$target 存档"
000242 |     } else {
000243 |         Remove-Item $DATA -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
000244 |         New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $DATA | Out-Null
000245 |         Log "账号$target 无存档，将以全新状态初始化"
000246 |     }
000247 |     $target | Out-File -Encoding ascii -NoNewline "$BASE\current.txt"
000248 |     Start-ClaudeApp
000249 |     Log "已切换到账号$target 并启动"
000250 |     "OK:已切换到账号$target"
000251 | }
000252 | 
000253 | switch ($args[0]) {
000254 |     'checkdetail' {
000255 |         $r = Invoke-CheckDetail
000256 |         $r | Out-File -Encoding utf8 "$BASE\last-check.txt"
000257 |         $r
000258 |     }
000259 |     'status' { Invoke-Status }
000260 |     'fix'    { Invoke-Fix }
000261 |     'switch' { Invoke-Switch $args[1] }
000262 |     default  { "用法: claude-switch.ps1 checkdetail|status|fix|switch A|B" }
000263 | }

000032 |         CheckRow(name: "ASN 属性", icon: "network"),
000033 |         CheckRow(name: "生态可达性", icon: "globe"),
000034 |     ]

000043 | 
000044 |     func run(_ args: [String], timeout: TimeInterval = 45, done: @escaping (String) -> Void) {
000045 |         DispatchQueue.global(qos: .userInitiated).async {
000046 |             let started = Date()
000047 |             let tmp = "/tmp/switcher-out-\(UUID().uuidString).txt"
000048 |             FileManager.default.createFile(atPath: tmp, contents: nil)
000049 |             let p = Process()
000050 |             p.launchPath = "/bin/bash"
000051 |             p.arguments = [self.core] + args
000052 |             if let fh = try? FileHandle(forWritingTo: URL(fileURLWithPath: tmp)) {
000053 |                 p.standardOutput = fh
000054 |                 p.standardError = fh
000055 |             }
000056 |             var timedOut = false
000057 |             let watchdog = DispatchWorkItem {
000058 |                 if p.isRunning { timedOut = true; p.terminate() }
000059 |             }
000060 |             DispatchQueue.global().asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + timeout, execute: watchdog)
000061 |             do { try p.run() } catch {
000062 |                 DispatchQueue.main.async { done("FAIL:无法启动脚本 \(error.localizedDescription)") }
000063 |                 return
000064 |             }
000065 |             p.waitUntilExit()
000066 |             watchdog.cancel()
000067 |             if let fh = p.standardOutput as? FileHandle { try? fh.close() }
000068 |             let data = (try? Data(contentsOf: URL(fileURLWithPath: tmp))) ?? Data()
000069 |             let out = String(decoding: data, as: UTF8.self)  // 容错解码:坏字节替换而非整体失败
000070 |             try? FileManager.default.removeItem(atPath: tmp)
000071 |             let dbg = "[\(Date())] args=\(args) timedOut=\(timedOut) exit=\(p.terminationStatus) 耗时\(String(format: "%.1f", Date().timeIntervalSince(started)))s stdout=\(out.count)字符\n"
000072 |             if let h = try? FileHandle(forWritingTo: URL(fileURLWithPath: "/tmp/switcher-debug.log")) {
000073 |                 h.seekToEndOfFile(); h.write(dbg.data(using: .utf8)!); h.closeFile()
000074 |             } else { try? dbg.write(toFile: "/tmp/switcher-debug.log", atomically: true, encoding: .utf8) }
000075 |             if timedOut {
000076 |                 DispatchQueue.main.async { done("FAIL:操作超时，请重试") }
000077 |                 return
000078 |             }
000079 |             DispatchQueue.main.async { done(out.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)) }
000080 |         }
000081 |     }
000082 | 
000083 |     func refresh() {
000084 |         phase = .checking
000085 |         banner = ""
000086 |         note = ""
000087 |         for i in rows.indices { rows[i].state = .pending; rows[i].detail = "" }
000088 |         run(["checkdetail"]) { out in
000089 |             // 输出为空时回退到脚本落盘的结果文件(双保险)
000090 |             var raw = out
000091 |             if raw.isEmpty, let diskData = try? Data(contentsOf: URL(fileURLWithPath: NSHomeDirectory() + "/.claude-switcher/last-check.txt")), !diskData.isEmpty {
000092 |                 raw = String(decoding: diskData, as: UTF8.self)
000093 |             }
000094 |             let out = raw
000095 |             var allOk = true
000096 |             var matched = 0
000097 |             let keyMap: [String: String] = [
000098 |                 "代理内核": "代理内核", "TUN网卡": "TUN 网卡", "出口IP": "出口 IP",
000099 |                 "IP一致": "IP 与上次一致", "时区": "时区", "地区": "地区",
000100 |                 "IPv6": "IPv6 关闭", "DNS": "DNS 走隧道", "WebRTC": "WebRTC",
000101 |                 "保镖": "保镖在线", "规则置顶": "规则置顶",
000102 |                 "客户端语言": "客户端语言", "ASN属性": "ASN 属性", "生态可达性": "生态可达性",
000103 |             ]
000104 |             for line in out.split(separator: "\n") {
000105 |                 let parts = line.split(separator: "=", maxSplits: 1).map(String.init)
000106 |                 guard parts.count == 2 else { continue }
000107 |                 let rhs = parts[1]
000108 |                 let status = rhs.split(separator: ":", maxSplits: 1).map(String.init)
000109 |                 let code = status[0]
000110 |                 let detail = status.count > 1 ? status[1] : ""
000111 |                 guard let rowName = keyMap[parts[0]], let idx = self.rows.firstIndex(where: { $0.name == rowName }) else { continue }
000112 |                 matched += 1
000113 |                 switch code {
000114 |                 case "ok": self.rows[idx].state = .ok
000115 |                 case "warn":
000116 |                     if ["IP一致", "ASN属性"].contains(parts[0]) {
000117 |                         self.rows[idx].state = .warn
000118 |                     } else {
000119 |                         self.rows[idx].state = .fail
000120 |                         allOk = false
000121 |                     }
000122 |                 default: self.rows[idx].state = .fail; allOk = false
000123 |                 }
000124 |                 self.rows[idx].detail = detail
000125 |             }
000126 |             self.run(["status"]) { st in
000127 |                 self.current = st.contains("current=B") ? "B" : (st.contains("current=A") ? "A" : "未初始化")
000128 |                 self.hasB = st.contains("hasB=1")
000129 |                 if matched < self.rows.count {
000130 |                     self.banner = "检测输出不完整(\(matched)/\(self.rows.count))，请点重新检查"
000131 |                     self.phase = .failed
000132 |                 } else {
000133 |                     self.phase = allOk ? .ready : .failed
000134 |                 }
000135 |             }
000136 |         }
000137 |     }
000138 | 
000139 |     func fixAll() {
000140 |         phase = .fixing
000141 |         banner = "正在修复…（若弹出密码框请输入开机密码）"
000142 |         run(["fix"], timeout: 180) { out in
000143 |             let steps = out.split(separator: "\n").map(String.init).filter { $0.hasPrefix("FIX:") }.map { String($0.dropFirst(4)) }
000144 |             self.banner = steps.last ?? ""
000145 |             self.refresh()
000146 |         }
000147 |     }
000148 | 
000149 |     func switchTo(_ target: String) {
000150 |         phase = .switching
000151 |         banner = "正在切换到账号 \(target)…"
000152 |         run(["switch", target], timeout: 180) { out in
000153 |             let outputLines = out.split(separator: "\n")
000154 |             if let failure = outputLines.last(where: { $0.hasPrefix("FAIL:") }) {
000155 |                 self.phase = .failed
000156 |                 self.banner = String(failure.dropFirst(5))
000157 |                 return
000158 |             }
000159 |             let alreadyCurrent = outputLines.contains(where: { $0.hasPrefix("INFO:") })
000160 |             var extra = ""
000161 |             if target == "B" && !self.hasB && !alreadyCurrent { extra = "B 号首次使用：请在打开的 Claude 里登录 B 账号，登录一次即可。" }
000162 |             self.current = target
000163 |             self.phase = .done
000164 |             self.banner = alreadyCurrent ? "当前已是账号 \(target)" : "已切换到账号 \(target)"
000165 |             self.note = extra
000166 |             DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 2.2) {
000167 |                 NSApplication.shared.terminate(nil)
000168 |             }
000169 |         }
000170 |     }
000171 | }

000001 | #!/bin/bash
000002 | # Claude 账号切换器 - 核心脚本 (通用版)
000003 | # 用法: claude-switch.sh check | status | switch A|B | checkdetail | fix | set-region | update [--auto] | diagnose-proxy
000004 | set -u
000005 | export LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8
000006 | 
000007 | # 随现有单脚本更新分发;在任何账号状态读写、审计/修复/启动之前分派。
000008 | if [ "${1:-}" = "diagnose-proxy" ]; then
000009 |     shift
000010 |     exec "${CLAUDE_SWITCHER_PYTHON:-python3}" - "$@" <<'PY_PROXY_DIAGNOSE'
000011 | """Read-only transport diagnostics for an explicitly selected HTTP/SOCKS proxy."""
000012 | import argparse
000013 | import concurrent.futures
000014 | import ipaddress
000015 | import json
000016 | import math
000017 | import os
000018 | import subprocess
000019 | from urllib.parse import urlsplit
000020 | 
000021 | 
000022 | TARGETS = (
000023 |     ("anthropic_api", "https://api.anthropic.com/v1/models"),
000024 |     ("claude_web", "https://claude.ai/"),
000025 | )
000026 | 
000027 | 
000028 | def select_proxy(args, environ):
000029 |     if args.proxy is not None:
000030 |         value, source = args.proxy, "argument"
000031 |     elif args.config is not None:
000032 |         with open(args.config, encoding="utf-8") as handle:
000033 |             config = json.load(handle)
000034 |         if not isinstance(config, dict):
000035 |             raise ValueError("invalid config")
000036 |         value, source = config.get("proxy_url"), "config"
000037 |     else:
000038 |         value, source = environ.get("CLAUDE_SWITCHER_PROXY_URL"), "environment"
000039 |     if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
000040 |         raise ValueError("proxy required")
000041 |     value = value.strip()
000042 |     if any(c.isspace() or ord(c) < 32 or ord(c) == 127 for c in value):
000043 |         raise ValueError("invalid proxy")
000044 |     parsed = urlsplit(value)
000045 |     if (parsed.scheme not in ("http", "https", "socks5", "socks5h")
000046 |             or not parsed.hostname or parsed.port is None
000047 |             or not 0 < parsed.port < 65536
000048 |             or parsed.path not in ("", "/") or parsed.query or parsed.fragment):
000049 |         raise ValueError("invalid proxy")
000050 |     try:
000051 |         loopback = ipaddress.ip_address(parsed.hostname).is_loopback
000052 |     except ValueError:
000053 |         loopback = parsed.hostname.lower() == "localhost"
000054 |     # Do not include proxy addresses, credentials, or private config paths in reports.
000055 |     public = {"scheme": parsed.scheme, "port": parsed.port,
000056 |               "host_scope": "loopback" if loopback else "remote",
000057 |               "authentication_configured": parsed.username is not None,
000058 |               "source": source}
000059 |     return value, public
000060 | 
000061 | 
000062 | def probe(name, url, proxy, environ):
000063 |     # -q ignores curlrc. Pass proxy credentials over stdin, never in argv/reports.
000064 |     quoted = proxy.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace('"', '\\"')
000065 |     config = 'proxy = "' + quoted + '"\n'
000066 |     args = ["curl", "-q", "--config", "-", "-sS", "-o", os.devnull,
000067 |             "--proto", "=https", "--noproxy", "", "--connect-timeout", "6",
000068 |             "--max-time", "12", "-w",
000069 |             "%{http_code}\t%{http_connect}\t%{ssl_verify_result}\t%{time_appconnect}\t%{time_total}",
000070 |             url]
000071 |     keep = {"PATH", "HOME", "TMPDIR", "LANG", "LC_ALL", "LC_CTYPE",
000072 |             "SSL_CERT_FILE", "SSL_CERT_DIR", "CURL_CA_BUNDLE", "SYSTEMROOT"}
000073 |     clean_env = {k: v for k, v in environ.items() if k in keep}
000074 |     result = {"name": name, "url": url, "transport_reached": False,
000075 |               "access": "unknown"}
000076 |     try:
000077 |         process = subprocess.run(args, input=config, env=clean_env, capture_output=True,
000078 |                                  text=True, timeout=15)
000079 |         fields = process.stdout.strip().split()
000080 |         status, connect, verify = map(int, fields[:3])
000081 |         tls, elapsed = map(float, fields[3:5])
000082 |         if (not 0 <= status <= 599 or not 0 <= connect <= 599 or verify < 0
000083 |                 or not math.isfinite(tls) or not math.isfinite(elapsed)
000084 |                 or tls < 0 or elapsed < 0):
000085 |             raise ValueError("invalid probe metrics")
000086 |         reached = process.returncode == 0 and status > 0 and verify == 0 and tls > 0
000087 |         access = ("unauthenticated" if status == 401 else
000088 |                   "access_restricted" if status in (403, 407) else
000089 |                   "server_error" if status >= 500 else "http_response")
000090 |         result.update(curl_exit=process.returncode, http_status=status,
000091 |                       proxy_connect_status=connect, tls_verified=reached,
000092 |                       elapsed_seconds=elapsed, transport_reached=reached,
000093 |                       access=access if reached else "unknown")
000094 |     except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
000095 |         result["error"] = "probe_timeout"
000096 |     except OSError:
000097 |         result["error"] = "curl_unavailable"
000098 |     except (ValueError, IndexError):
000099 |         result["error"] = "invalid_probe_output"
000100 |     return result
000101 | 
000102 | 
000103 | class DiagnosticParser(argparse.ArgumentParser):
000104 |     def error(self, message):
000105 |         # argparse's default error can echo private URLs or paths from argv.
000106 |         raise ValueError("invalid arguments")
000107 | 
000108 | 
000109 | def main(argv=None, environ=None):
000110 |     parser = DiagnosticParser(description=__doc__)
000111 |     choice = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
000112 |     choice.add_argument("--proxy", help="explicit proxy URL including a port")
000113 |     choice.add_argument("--config", help="private JSON file containing proxy_url")
000114 |     environ = os.environ if environ is None else environ
000115 |     report = {"schema_version": 1, "mode": "proxy_diagnostics",
000116 |               "scope": "unauthenticated_transport_only",
000117 |               "account_status": "not_evaluated", "leak_protection": "not_evaluated",
000118 |               "launch_allowed": None, "application_started": False,
000119 |               "system_modified": False}
000120 |     try:
000121 |         args = parser.parse_args(argv)
000122 |         proxy, public = select_proxy(args, environ)
000123 |     except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
000124 |         report.update(state="configuration_error", error="invalid_or_missing_proxy",
000125 |                       hint="Use --proxy, --config, or CLAUDE_SWITCHER_PROXY_URL; an explicit port is required.")
000126 |         print(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False))
000127 |         return 2
000128 |     with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
000129 |         futures = [pool.submit(probe, name, url, proxy, environ) for name, url in TARGETS]
000130 |         checks = [f.result() for f in futures]
000131 |     reached = all(check["transport_reached"] for check in checks)
000132 |     report.update(proxy=public, checks=checks,
000133 |                   state="transport_reached" if reached else "transport_failed")
000134 |     print(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False))
000135 |     return 0 if reached else 1
000136 | 
000137 | 
000138 | if __name__ == "__main__":
000139 |     raise SystemExit(main())
000140 | PY_PROXY_DIAGNOSE
000141 | fi

000187 | # Shadowrocket 在 Mac 上表现为 VPN 配置;launchd/非图形环境下 scutil 可能查不到该行,此时以 TUN 判定为准(避免误杀)
000188 | shadowrocket_up() {
000189 |     nc=$(scutil --nc list 2>/dev/null)
000190 |     if echo "$nc" | grep -q 'com.liguangming.Shadowrocket'; then
000191 |         echo "$nc" | grep 'com.liguangming.Shadowrocket' | grep -q '(Connected)'
000192 |     else
000193 |         tun_up
000194 |     fi
000195 | }

000197 | # 代理内核在线判定:按安装时识别的客户端分派;auto 时任一命中即活
000198 | proxy_alive() {
000199 |     case "$PROXY_MODE" in
000200 |         clash-verge)  pgrep -f verge-mihomo >/dev/null 2>&1 ;;
000201 |         shadowrocket) shadowrocket_up ;;
000202 |         sing-box)     pgrep -f sing-box >/dev/null 2>&1 ;;
000203 |         clashx)       pgrep -x ClashX >/dev/null 2>&1 || pgrep -f clash >/dev/null 2>&1 ;;
000204 |         surge)        pgrep -f Surge >/dev/null 2>&1 ;;
000205 |         auto)         pgrep -f verge-mihomo >/dev/null 2>&1 || pgrep -x 0dcloudCore >/dev/null 2>&1 || shadowrocket_up \
000206 |                       || pgrep -f sing-box >/dev/null 2>&1 \
000207 |                       || pgrep -x ClashX >/dev/null 2>&1 || pgrep -f clash >/dev/null 2>&1 \
000208 |                       || pgrep -f Surge >/dev/null 2>&1 ;;
000209 |         *)            pgrep -f verge-mihomo >/dev/null 2>&1 ;;
000210 |     esac
000211 | }

000218 | # 置顶规则行为检测:三个生态域名全部解析到 fake-ip 段(198.18.x.x)即视为已置顶;未命中域名记入 ROUTE_MISSED
000219 | route_ok() {

000232 | # 国家代码 → 地区格式映射(未收录的国家回退 en_US)
000233 | locale_for() {
000234 |     case "$1" in
000235 |         US) echo en_US ;; JP) echo ja_JP ;; TW) echo zh_TW ;; HK) echo zh_HK ;;
000236 |         SG) echo en_SG ;; GB) echo en_GB ;; DE) echo de_DE ;; FR) echo fr_FR ;;
000237 |         KR) echo ko_KR ;; AU) echo en_AU ;; CA) echo en_CA ;; *) echo en_US ;;
000238 |     esac
000239 | }

000241 | # 国家与时区优先从 ipinfo 获取;遇到限流或无效响应时回退到 ipwho.is。
000242 | lookup_geo() {
000243 |     local url payload fields
000244 |     for url in https://ipinfo.io/json https://ipwho.is/ https://api.ip.sb/geoip; do
000245 |         payload=$(curl -fsS --max-time 8 "$url" 2>/dev/null) || continue
000246 |         fields=$(printf '%s' "$payload" | python3 -c '
000247 | import json, sys
000248 | import ipaddress
000249 | try:
000250 |     data = json.load(sys.stdin)
000251 |     country = data.get("country_code") or data.get("country") or ""
000252 |     zone = data.get("timezone") or ""
000253 |     if isinstance(zone, dict):
000254 |         zone = zone.get("id", "")
000255 |     address = data.get("ip") or ""
000256 |     ipaddress.ip_address(address)
000257 |     if len(country) == 2 and zone:
000258 |         print(country + "\t" + zone + "\t" + address)
000259 | except Exception:
000260 |     pass
000261 | ' 2>/dev/null)
000262 |         if [ -n "$fields" ]; then printf '%s\n' "$fields"; return 0; fi
000263 |     done
000264 |     return 1
000265 | }
000266 | 
000267 | # Cloudflare trace 优先;不可达时用已校验的地理服务结果提供出口 IP 与国家。
000268 | lookup_egress() {
000269 |     local trace address country geo zone
000270 |     trace=$(curl -fsS --max-time 8 https://www.cloudflare.com/cdn-cgi/trace 2>/dev/null) || trace=""
000271 |     address=$(printf '%s\n' "$trace" | sed -n 's/^ip=//p' | head -1)
000272 |     country=$(printf '%s\n' "$trace" | sed -n 's/^loc=//p' | head -1)
000273 |     if [ -n "$address" ] && [[ "$country" =~ ^[A-Z]{2}$ ]]; then
000274 |         printf '%s\t%s\n' "$address" "$country"
000275 |         return 0
000276 |     fi
000277 |     geo=$(lookup_geo) || return 1
000278 |     IFS=$'\t' read -r country zone address <<< "$geo"
000279 |     [ -n "$address" ] && [[ "$country" =~ ^[A-Z]{2}$ ]] || return 1
000280 |     printf '%s\t%s\n' "$address" "$country"
000281 | }
000282 | 
000283 | # IP 审查:记录出口IP并统计最近48h内不同IP个数(账号画像风险指标)
000284 | iphist_count() {
000285 |     [ -z "$1" ] && { echo 0; return; }
000286 |     local HIST="$BASE/iphistory.log"
000287 |     local now=$(date +%s)
000288 |     echo "$1 $now" >>"$HIST"
000289 |     # 裁掉48小时(172800秒)以前的记录
000290 |     awk -v cutoff=$((now-172800)) '$2>=cutoff' "$HIST" > "$HIST.tmp" && mv "$HIST.tmp" "$HIST"
000291 |     # 统计48h内不同IP个数
000292 |     awk '{print $1}' "$HIST" | sort -u | wc -l | tr -d ' '
000293 | }

000295 | # ---------- 安全检查 ----------
000296 | check() {
000297 |     fails=""
000298 | 
000299 |     proxy_alive || fails="$fails;代理内核未运行"
000300 |     tun_up || fails="$fails;TUN网卡不在线"
000301 | 
000302 |     egress=$(lookup_egress) || egress=""
000303 |     IFS=$'\t' read -r ip loc <<< "$egress"
000304 |     [ "$loc" = "$COUNTRY_TARGET" ] || fails="$fails;出口IP异常(${ip:-无}/${loc:-未知},期望$COUNTRY_TARGET)"
000305 |     ipwarn=""
000306 |     lastip=""; [ -f "$BASE/lastip" ] && lastip=$(cat "$BASE/lastip")
000307 |     if [ -n "$ip" ] && [ -n "$lastip" ] && [ "$ip" != "$lastip" ]; then
000308 |         ipwarn="注意:出口IP与上次不同($lastip -> $ip)"
000309 |     fi
000310 |     [ -n "$ip" ] && echo "$ip" > "$BASE/lastip"
000311 | 
000312 |     # IP 审查:48h 内出口 IP 变化 ≥3 次追加警告(只 warn 不 fail)
000313 |     ipn=$(iphist_count "$ip")
000314 |     if [ "$ipn" -ge 3 ] 2>/dev/null; then
000315 |         ipwarn="${ipwarn:+$ipwarn; }注意:48h内出口IP变化${ipn}次,账号画像有风险,建议固定节点"
000316 |     fi
000317 | 
000318 |     tz=$(readlink /etc/localtime 2>/dev/null)
000319 |     [ "$tz" = "/var/db/timezone/zoneinfo/$TZ_TARGET" ] || fails="$fails;时区异常($tz)"
000320 |

000322 |     [ "$locale" = "$LOCALE_TARGET" ] || fails="$fails;地区异常($locale)"

000326 |

000328 |     case "$dnsip" in
000329 |         198.18.*) ;;
000330 |         *) fails="$fails;DNS未走隧道(返回${dnsip:-空})" ;;
000331 |     esac
000332 | 
000333 |     wr=$(webrtc_test)
000334 |     if [ "$wr" != "RESULT:NONE" ]; then
000335 |         case "$wr" in
000336 |             RESULT:*)
000337 |                 wips=${wr#RESULT:}
000338 |                 leak=""
000339 |                 OLDIFS=$IFS; IFS=','
000340 |                 for w in $wips; do [ "$w" != "$ip" ] && leak="$leak $w"; done
000341 |                 IFS=$OLDIFS
000342 |                 [ -n "$leak" ] && fails="$fails;WebRTC泄漏($leak)" ;;
000343 |             *) fails="$fails;WebRTC检测异常" ;;
000344 |         esac
000345 |     fi
000346 | 
000347 |     launchctl print system/local.claude-bodyguard >/dev/null 2>&1 || fails="$fails;保镖未运行"
000348 | 
000349 |     # 置顶规则:Clash Verge 看配置或行为任一通过;其他客户端只看行为检测
000350 |     if [ "$PROXY_MODE" = "clash-verge" ]; then
000351 |         merge_ok || route_ok || fails="$fails;置顶规则缺失"
000352 |     else
000353 |         route_ok || fails="$fails;置顶规则缺失(未命中:${ROUTE_MISSED# })"
000354 |     fi
000355 | 
000356 |     defaults read com.anthropic.claudefordesktop AppleLanguages 2>/dev/null | grep -q 'en-US' || {
000357 |         syslang=$(defaults read -g AppleLanguages 2>/dev/null | sed -n '2p' | tr -d ' ,"')
000358 |         [[ "$syslang" == en* ]] || fails="$fails;客户端语言为中文"
000359 |     }
000360 | 
000361 |     r1=$(curl -s --max-time 8 -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' https://claude.ai/ 2>/dev/null)
000362 |     r2=$(curl -s --max-time 8 -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' https://api.anthropic.com/v1/models 2>/dev/null)
000363 |     { [ "$r1" = "000" ] || [ "$r2" = "000" ]; } && fails="$fails;Anthropic生态不可达($r1/$r2)"
000364 | 
000365 |     if [ -n "$fails" ]; then
000366 |         echo "FAIL:${fails#;}"
000367 |         return 1
000368 |     fi
000369 |     echo "OK:$ip${ipwarn:+|$ipwarn}"
000370 |     return 0
000371 | }

000380 |     if proxy_alive; then

000387 |     fi

000389 |     egress=$(lookup_egress) || egress=""
000390 |     IFS=$'\t' read -r ip loc <<< "$egress"
000391 |     if [ "$loc" = "$COUNTRY_TARGET" ]; then echo "出口IP=ok:$ip/$loc"; else echo "出口IP=fail:${ip:-无}/${loc:-未知}(期望$COUNTRY_TARGET)"; fi
000392 |     lastip=""; [ -f "$BASE/lastip" ] && lastip=$(cat "$BASE/lastip")
000393 |     ipst=""; ipdt=""
000394 |     if [ -z "$ip" ]; then ipst="fail"; ipdt="未获取到出口IP"
000395 |     elif [ -z "$lastip" ]; then ipst="ok"; ipdt="首次记录"
000396 |     elif [ "$ip" = "$lastip" ]; then ipst="ok"
000397 |     else ipst="warn"; ipdt="$lastip → $ip"; fi
000398 |     [ -n "$ip" ] && echo "$ip" > "$BASE/lastip"
000399 |     # IP 审查:48h 内出口 IP 变化 ≥3 次提示账号画像风险(只 warn 不 fail)
000400 |     if [ -n "$ipst" ]; then
000401 |         ipn=$(iphist_count "$ip")

000403 |             if [ "$ipst" = "ok" ]; then
000404 |                 ipst="warn"; ipdt="48h内出口IP变化${ipn}次,账号画像有风险,建议固定节点"
000405 |             else
000406 |                 ipdt="$ipdt;48h内出口IP变化${ipn}次,账号画像有风险,建议固定节点"
000407 |             fi
000408 |         fi
000409 |         if [ -z "$ipdt" ]; then echo "IP一致=$ipst"; else echo "IP一致=$ipst:$ipdt"; fi
000410 |     fi

000412 |     if [ "$tz" = "/var/db/timezone/zoneinfo/$TZ_TARGET" ]; then echo '时区=ok'; else echo "时区=fail:$tz"; fi

000418 |     case "$dnsip" in
000419 |         198.18.*) echo 'DNS=ok:走隧道' ;;
000420 |         *) echo "DNS=fail:未走隧道(${dnsip:-空})" ;;
000421 |     esac
000422 |     wr=$(webrtc_test)
000423 |     case "$wr" in
000424 |         RESULT:NONE) echo 'WebRTC=ok:UDP不出网,零暴露' ;;
000425 |         RESULT:*)
000426 |             wips=${wr#RESULT:}
000427 |             leak=""
000428 |             OLDIFS=$IFS; IFS=','
000429 |             for w in $wips; do [ "$w" != "$ip" ] && leak="$leak $w"; done
000430 |             IFS=$OLDIFS
000431 |             if [ -n "$leak" ]; then echo "WebRTC=fail:暴露非代理IP:$leak"; else echo 'WebRTC=ok:仅暴露代理IP'; fi ;;
000432 |         *) echo 'WebRTC=fail:检测异常' ;;
000433 |     esac
000434 |     if launchctl print system/local.claude-bodyguard >/dev/null 2>&1; then
000435 |         echo '保镖=ok:守护进程运行中'
000436 |     else
000437 |         echo '保镖=fail:未安装'
000438 |     fi
000439 |     # 置顶规则:Clash Verge 配置/行为双保险,其他客户端只看行为
000440 |     if [ "$PROXY_MODE" = "clash-verge" ]; then
000441 |         if merge_ok; then
000442 |             echo '规则置顶=ok'
000443 |         elif route_ok; then
000444 |             echo '规则置顶=ok:行为检测通过'
000445 |         else
000446 |             echo '规则置顶=fail:未生效'
000447 |         fi
000448 |     else
000449 |         if route_ok; then
000450 |             echo '规则置顶=ok:行为检测通过'
000451 |         else
000452 |             echo "规则置顶=fail:未命中(${ROUTE_MISSED# }),请在 $(proxy_name) 中自行加置顶规则"
000453 |         fi
000454 |     fi
000455 |     # 12. Claude 客户端语言(单独设为英文,或系统首选语言为英文均可;防止 JS 层读出中文)
000456 |     clang=$(defaults read com.anthropic.claudefordesktop AppleLanguages 2>/dev/null | head -2)

000458 |     if echo "$clang" | grep -q 'en-US'; then

000460 |     elif [[ "$syslang" == en* ]]; then

000462 |     else

000488 | # 检查输出必须含 14 个唯一项目。warn 项允许继续，缺项和未知状态一律阻断。
000489 | validate_audit_output() {
000490 |     local output="$1" total key row state line prefix found problems="" expected=14
000491 |     local -a keys=("代理内核" "TUN网卡" "出口IP" "IP一致" "时区" "地区" "IPv6" "DNS" \
000492 |         "WebRTC" "保镖" "规则置顶" "客户端语言" "ASN属性" "生态可达性")
000493 |     total=0
000494 |     while IFS= read -r line; do
000495 |         [ -z "$line" ] && continue
000496 |         [[ "$line" == \#* ]] && continue
000497 |         total=$((total + 1))
000498 |     done <<< "$output"
000499 |     [ "$total" -eq "$expected" ] || problems="审计行数异常($total/$expected)"
000500 |     for key in "${keys[@]}"; do
000501 |         row=""; found=0
000502 |         while IFS= read -r line; do
000503 |             [ -z "$line" ] && continue
000504 |             [[ "$line" == \#* ]] && continue
000505 |             prefix=${line%%=*}
000506 |             if [ "$prefix" = "$key" ]; then row="$line"; found=$((found + 1)); fi
000507 |         done <<< "$output"
000508 |         if [ "$found" -ne 1 ]; then
000509 |             problems="${problems:+$problems; }缺少或重复检查项:$key"
000510 |             continue
000511 |         fi
000512 |         state=${row#*=}
000513 |         state=${state%%:*}
000514 |         case "$state" in
000515 |             ok) ;;
000516 |             warn)
000517 |                 case "$key" in
000518 |                     IP一致|ASN属性) ;;
000519 |                     *) problems="${problems:+$problems; }不允许的警告:$key" ;;
000520 |                 esac ;;
000521 |             *) problems="${problems:+$problems; }$key=${state:-未知状态}" ;;
000522 |         esac
000523 |     done
000524 |     AUDIT_ERRORS="$problems"
000525 |     [ -z "$problems" ]
000526 | }
000527 | 
000528 | # Claude 启动前必须收到完整的 14 项审计结果。
000529 | launch_preflight() {
000530 |     local output
000531 |     output=$(checkdetail 2>&1)
000532 |     printf '%s\n' "$output"
000533 |     if ! validate_audit_output "$output"; then
000534 |         echo "FAIL:启动被阻止，环境审计未通过：$AUDIT_ERRORS"
000535 |         return 1
000536 |     fi
000537 |     echo "OK:14项环境审计通过"
000538 | }
000539 | 
000540 | # 置顶规则写入(智能合并):不整文件覆盖,用户已有的 Merge 配置原样保留
000541 | # 标记块自包含 prepend-rules 键+全部规则,重复执行幂等
000542 | write_merge_rules() {
000543 |     M="$CLASH_DIR/profiles/Merge.yaml"
000544 |     B0="# >>> claude-switcher pinned rules >>>"
000545 |     B1="# <<< claude-switcher pinned rules <<<"
000546 |     mkdir -p "$CLASH_DIR/profiles"
000547 |     # 置顶规则清单(缩进两格)
000548 |     rules=""
000549 |     for d in claude.ai claude.com anthropic.com claudeusercontent.com challenges.cloudflare.com datadoghq.com segment.io stripe.com stripe.network stripecdn.com intercom.io intercomcdn.com intercomassets.com; do
000550 |         rules="$rules  - DOMAIN-SUFFIX,$d,$PROXY_GROUP
000551 | "
000552 |     done
000553 | 
000554 |     # 首次:文件不存在,整文件写入
000555 |     if [ ! -f "$M" ]; then
000556 |         cat > "$M" <<MEOF
000557 | # Profile Enhancement Merge Template for Clash Verge
000558 | 
000559 | profile:
000560 |   store-selected: true
000561 | 
000562 | # Claude 全家族及生态域名置顶强制走代理(优先级高于任何国内规则集)
000563 | $B0
000564 | prepend-rules:
000565 | $rules$B1
000566 | MEOF
000567 |         return 0
000568 |     fi
000569 | 
000570 |     # 已存在:备份后幂等替换(只在将要修改时备份)
000571 |     cp "$M" "$M.bak.$(date +%Y%m%d%H%M%S)"
000572 |     tmp="$M.cs.tmp"
000573 |     cp "$M" "$tmp"
000574 |     # 删除旧标记块(含标记行,兼容块在 prepend-rules 下缩进的情形)
000575 |     if grep -qF "$B0" "$tmp"; then
000576 |         awk -v b0="$B0" -v b1="$B1" '
000577 |             index($0, b0) {inblock=1; next}
000578 |             inblock && index($0, b1) {inblock=0; next}
000579 |             !inblock
000580 |         ' "$tmp" > "$tmp.out" && mv "$tmp.out" "$tmp"
000581 |         # 清理孤立的 prepend-rules: 键(其后没有规则行,只剩空行/注释/EOF)
000582 |         awk '
000583 |             { L[NR]=$0 }
000584 |             END {
000585 |                 for (i=1; i<=NR; i++) {
000586 |                     if (L[i] ~ /^prepend-rules:/) {
000587 |                         has=0
000588 |                         for (j=i+1; j<=NR; j++) {
000589 |                             if (L[j] ~ /^[ \t]*- /) { has=1; break }
000590 |                             if (L[j] ~ /^[ \t]*$/ || L[j] ~ /^[ \t]*#/) continue
000591 |                             break
000592 |                         }
000593 |                         if (!has) continue
000594 |                     }
000595 |                     print L[i]
000596 |                 }
000597 |             }
000598 |         ' "$tmp" > "$tmp.out" && mv "$tmp.out" "$tmp"
000599 |     fi
000600 |     # 注入:已有 prepend-rules: 则标记规则块(不带键)插到该行之后,否则文件顶部插入完整块
000601 |     # 注意:多行文本走环境变量传递(macOS awk 的 -v 不支持含换行的字符串);先写中间文件再替换,失败不碰原文件
000602 |     if grep -q '^prepend-rules:' "$tmp"; then
000603 |         if MARK0="$B0" MARK1="$B1" RULES="$rules" awk '
000604 |             /^prepend-rules:/ && !done {
000605 |                 print
000606 |                 print "  " ENVIRON["MARK0"]
000607 |                 printf "%s", ENVIRON["RULES"]
000608 |                 print "  " ENVIRON["MARK1"]
000609 |                 done=1
000610 |                 next
000611 |             }
000612 |             { print }
000613 |         ' "$tmp" > "$tmp.out"; then
000614 |             mv "$tmp.out" "$M" && rm -f "$tmp"
000615 |         else
000616 |             rm -f "$tmp" "$tmp.out"
000617 |             return 1
000618 |         fi
000619 |     else
000620 |         {
000621 |             echo "# Claude 全家族及生态域名置顶强制走代理(优先级高于任何国内规则集)"
000622 |             echo "$B0"
000623 |             echo "prepend-rules:"
000624 |             printf "%s" "$rules"
000625 |             echo "$B1"
000626 |             echo ""
000627 |             cat "$tmp"
000628 |         } > "$tmp.out" && mv "$tmp.out" "$M" && rm -f "$tmp"
000629 |     fi
000630 |     rm -f "$tmp"
000631 | }

000633 | # 一键修复:按项自动修复,每步输出结果
000634 | fix() {
000635 |     echo "FIX:开始一键修复"
000636 | 
000637 |     # A. 代理内核 + TUN
000638 |     if [ "$PROXY_MODE" = "clash-verge" ]; then
000639 |         if ! pgrep -f verge-mihomo >/dev/null 2>&1; then
000640 |             echo "FIX:正在启动 Clash Verge..."
000641 |             open -a "Clash Verge"
000642 |         fi
000643 |         for i in $(seq 1 40); do
000644 |             pgrep -f verge-mihomo >/dev/null 2>&1 && tun_up && break
000645 |             sleep 1
000646 |         done
000647 |         if pgrep -f verge-mihomo >/dev/null 2>&1 && tun_up; then
000648 |             echo "FIX:代理内核+TUN ✓"
000649 |         else
000650 |             V="$CLASH_DIR/verge.yaml"
000651 |             if [ -f "$V" ] && grep -q '^enable_tun_mode: false' "$V"; then
000652 |                 echo "FIX:检测到 TUN 开关被关闭,正在恢复..."
000653 |                 osascript -e 'tell application "Clash Verge" to quit' >/dev/null 2>&1
000654 |                 sleep 3
000655 |                 sed -i '' 's/^enable_tun_mode: false/enable_tun_mode: true/' "$V"
000656 |                 open -a "Clash Verge"
000657 |                 for i in $(seq 1 40); do
000658 |                     tun_up && break
000659 |                     sleep 1
000660 |                 done
000661 |             fi
000662 |             if pgrep -f verge-mihomo >/dev/null 2>&1 && tun_up; then
000663 |                 echo "FIX:代理内核+TUN ✓(已恢复TUN开关)"
000664 |             else
000665 |                 echo "FIX:代理内核/TUN ✗(请打开 Clash Verge 检查节点/线路)"
000666 |             fi
000667 |         fi
000668 |     else
000669 |         app=$(proxy_app_name)
000670 |         if ! proxy_alive && [ -n "$app" ]; then
000671 |             echo "FIX:正在启动 $app..."
000672 |             open -a "$app" >/dev/null 2>&1
000673 |         fi
000674 |         for i in $(seq 1 40); do
000675 |             proxy_alive && tun_up && break
000676 |             sleep 1
000677 |         done
000678 |         if proxy_alive && tun_up; then
000679 |             echo "FIX:代理内核+TUN ✓"
000680 |         else
000681 |             echo "FIX:代理内核/TUN ✗(请打开 $(proxy_name) 手动检查节点/线路及TUN模式)"
000682 |         fi
000683 |     fi
000684 | 
000685 |     # 禁止在隧道或目标地区未确认时改动系统时区、IPv6 或守护服务。
000686 |     if ! proxy_alive || ! tun_up; then
000687 |         echo "FIX:已停止；代理内核与 TUN 必须同时在线后才会修改系统设置"
000688 |         return 1
000689 |     fi
000690 |     egress=$(lookup_egress) || egress=""
000691 |     IFS=$'\t' read -r ip loc <<< "$egress"
000692 |     if [ -z "$ip" ] || [ "$loc" != "$COUNTRY_TARGET" ]; then
000693 |         echo "FIX:已停止；出口国家未确认($loc)，目标为$COUNTRY_TARGET"
000694 |         return 1
000695 |     fi
000696 | 
000697 |     # B. 时区/IPv6/DNS缓存/保镖(需管理员权限,会弹一次密码框)
000698 |     tz=$(readlink /etc/localtime 2>/dev/null)
000699 |     v6=$(networksetup -getinfo Wi-Fi 2>/dev/null | grep -i '^IPv6:')
000700 |     adminCmds=""
000701 |     [ "$tz" != "/var/db/timezone/zoneinfo/$TZ_TARGET" ] && adminCmds="${adminCmds}/usr/sbin/systemsetup -settimezone $TZ_TARGET; "
000702 |     echo "$v6" | grep -qi 'off' || adminCmds="${adminCmds}networksetup -setv6off Wi-Fi; "
000703 |     if ! launchctl print system/local.claude-bodyguard >/dev/null 2>&1; then
000704 |         adminCmds="${adminCmds}cp $HOME/.local/bin/local.claude-bodyguard.plist /Library/LaunchDaemons/ && chown root:wheel /Library/LaunchDaemons/local.claude-bodyguard.plist && launchctl bootstrap system /Library/LaunchDaemons/local.claude-bodyguard.plist; "
000705 |     fi
000706 |     if [ -n "$adminCmds" ]; then
000707 |         adminCmds="${adminCmds}dscacheutil -flushcache; killall -HUP mDNSResponder"
000708 |         echo "FIX:修复时区/IPv6/DNS/保镖(需要密码)..."
000709 |         if osascript <<ASEOF >/dev/null 2>&1
000710 | do shell script "$adminCmds" with prompt "一键修复需要管理员权限" with administrator privileges
000711 | ASEOF
000712 |         then
000713 |             echo "FIX:时区/IPv6/DNS/保镖 ✓"
000714 |         else
000715 |             echo "FIX:时区/IPv6/保镖 ✗(取消或失败)"
000716 |         fi
000717 |     else
000718 |         echo "FIX:时区/IPv6/保镖 无需修复 ✓"
000719 |     fi
000720 |

000722 |     syslang=$(defaults read -g AppleLanguages 2>/dev/null | sed -n '2p' | tr -d ' ,"')
000723 |     if ! defaults read com.anthropic.claudefordesktop AppleLanguages 2>/dev/null | grep -q 'en-US' && [[ "$syslang" != en* ]]; then
000724 |         defaults write com.anthropic.claudefordesktop AppleLanguages -array en-US
000725 |         echo "FIX:Claude 客户端语言 → en-US ✓(重启 Claude 后生效)"
000726 |     fi

000730 |     if [ "$locale" != "$LOCALE_TARGET" ]; then
000731 |         defaults write -g AppleLocale "$LOCALE_TARGET"
000732 |         echo "FIX:地区格式 → $LOCALE_TARGET ✓"
000733 |     fi
000734 | 
000735 |     # B2. 置顶规则缺失 → Clash Verge 智能合并写入 Merge 配置并重启;其他客户端提示自行配置
000736 |     if [ "$PROXY_MODE" = "clash-verge" ]; then
000737 |         if ! merge_ok && ! route_ok; then

000739 |             write_merge_rules

000741 |             sleep 3

000744 |                 tun_up && break

000746 |             done

000748 |                 && echo "FIX:置顶规则 ✓" || echo "FIX:置顶规则 ✗"
000749 |         fi
000750 |     else
000751 |         if ! route_ok; then
000752 |             echo "FIX:置顶规则 ✗(未命中:${ROUTE_MISSED# }) — 请在 $(proxy_name) 中自行添加 DOMAIN-SUFFIX 置顶规则"
000753 |         fi
000754 |     fi
000755 | 
000756 |     # C. 出口IP最终验证
000757 |     sleep 2
000758 |     egress=$(lookup_egress) || egress=""
000759 |     IFS=$'\t' read -r ip loc <<< "$egress"
000760 |     if [ "$loc" = "$COUNTRY_TARGET" ]; then
000761 |         echo "FIX:出口IP ✓ ($ip/$loc)"
000762 |     else
000763 |         echo "FIX:出口IP ✗ (${ip:-无}/${loc:-未知},期望$COUNTRY_TARGET) — 可能是节点故障,请到 $(proxy_name) 里换条线路"
000764 |     fi

000768 | # 地区自适应:按当前出口国家一键重设 国家/时区/地区格式(换了国家节点后运行)
000769 | set_region() {
000770 |     trace=$(curl -fsS --max-time 8 https://www.cloudflare.com/cdn-cgi/trace 2>/dev/null) || trace=""
000771 |     loc=$(printf '%s\n' "$trace" | sed -n 's/^loc=//p' | head -1)
000772 |     geo=$(lookup_geo) || { echo "FAIL:地区服务未返回国家或时区"; return 1; }
000773 |     IFS=$'\t' read -r ctry ntz geoip <<< "$geo"
000774 |     if [ -n "$loc" ] && [ "$loc" != "$ctry" ]; then
000775 |         echo "FAIL:出口国家不一致(cloudflare=$loc 地理服务=$ctry),已中止"; return 1
000776 |     fi
000777 |     nlocale=$(locale_for "$ctry")
000778 | 
000779 |     # 改写脚本自身头部三个配置项
000780 |     sed -i '' \
000781 |         -e "s|^COUNTRY_TARGET=\".*\"|COUNTRY_TARGET=\"$ctry\"|" \
000782 |         -e "s|^TZ_TARGET=\".*\"|TZ_TARGET=\"$ntz\"|" \
000783 |         -e "s|^LOCALE_TARGET=\".*\"|LOCALE_TARGET=\"$nlocale\"|" \
000784 |         "$0"
000785 |     COUNTRY_TARGET="$ctry"; TZ_TARGET="$ntz"; LOCALE_TARGET="$nlocale"
000786 | 
000787 |     # 地区格式(用户级,无需密码)
000788 |     defaults write -g AppleLocale "$nlocale"
000789 | 
000790 |     # 时区(需管理员权限,会弹一次授权框);看门狗脚本在 root 目录下,顺道用同一次授权同步目标值
000791 |     WD="/Library/Application Support/claude-switcher/tz-watchdog.sh"
000792 |     adminCmds="/usr/sbin/systemsetup -settimezone $ntz"
000793 |     wdsync=0
000794 |     if [ -f "$WD" ] && [ ! -w "$WD" ]; then
000795 |         adminCmds="$adminCmds; /usr/bin/sed -i '' 's|^TARGET=.*|TARGET=$ntz|' '$WD'"
000796 |         wdsync=1
000797 |     fi
000798 |     if ! osascript <<ASEOF >/dev/null 2>&1
000799 | do shell script "$adminCmds" with prompt "set-region 需要管理员权限以锁定时区" with administrator privileges
000800 | ASEOF
000801 |     then
000802 |         echo "FAIL:时区设置被取消或失败(国家/地区格式已更新为 $ctry/$nlocale)"
000803 |         return 1
000804 |     fi
000805 |     # 看门狗目标同步兜底(可写的老版本安装直接 sed;失败则提示运行安装器刷新)
000806 |     if [ -f "$WD" ] && [ "$wdsync" -eq 0 ]; then
000807 |         sed -i '' "s|^TARGET=.*|TARGET=$ntz|" "$WD" 2>/dev/null || echo "WARN:看门狗目标未同步,请运行安装器刷新"
000808 |     fi
000809 | 
000810 |     echo "OK:region=$loc tz=$ntz locale=$nlocale"
000811 | }

000813 | # 自动更新:拉取 main 分支最新代码,SHA256 校验+配置注入+自检+失败回滚(--auto 供 LaunchAgent 每12小时调用)
000814 | self_update() {
000815 |     if [ "${1:-}" = "--auto" ]; then auto=1; else auto=0; fi
000816 |     # 路径全部从脚本自身位置推导,兼容旧版 LaunchDaemon(root) 混跑时 $HOME 不可靠的场景
000817 |     BIN=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)
000818 |     UHOME="${BIN%/.local/bin}"
000819 |     [ "$UHOME" = "$BIN" ] && UHOME="$HOME"
000820 |     if [ "$auto" -eq 1 ]; then
000821 |         mkdir -p "$BASE"
000822 |         exec >>"$BASE/update.log" 2>&1
000823 |         echo "# $(date '+%F %T') update --auto 开始"
000824 |     fi
000825 | 
000826 |     tmpd=$(mktemp -d)
000827 |     if ! curl -sL --max-time 30 "https://codeload.github.com/517183415-dev/claude-switcher/zip/refs/heads/main" -o "$tmpd/main.zip" \
000828 |         || ! unzip -q "$tmpd/main.zip" -d "$tmpd" 2>/dev/null; then
000829 |         echo "FAIL:下载或解压失败(网络问题,稍后再试)"; rm -rf "$tmpd"; return 1
000830 |     fi
000831 |     NEW="$tmpd/claude-switcher-main"
000832 |     if [ ! -f "$NEW/core/claude-switch.sh" ]; then
000833 |         echo "FAIL:远程包结构异常,已中止"; rm -rf "$tmpd"; return 1
000834 |     fi
000835 | 
000836 |     # 工件完整性校验:逐项比对仓库 SHA256SUMS,任一不符即中止(防传输篡改;信任根为 GitHub 仓库本身)
000837 |     if ! curl -sL --max-time 15 "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/517183415-dev/claude-switcher/main/SHA256SUMS" -o "$tmpd/SHA256SUMS" \
000838 |         || [ ! -s "$tmpd/SHA256SUMS" ]; then
000839 |         echo "FAIL:无法获取校验清单(SHA256SUMS),已中止"; rm -rf "$tmpd"; return 1
000840 |     fi
000841 |     bad=0
000842 |     while read -r sum rel; do
000843 |         [ -z "$sum" ] && continue
000844 |         if [ ! -f "$NEW/$rel" ] || [ "$(shasum -a 256 "$NEW/$rel" | awk '{print $1}')" != "$sum" ]; then
000845 |             bad=1; break
000846 |         fi
000847 |     done < "$tmpd/SHA256SUMS"
000848 |     if [ "$bad" -eq 1 ]; then
000849 |         echo "FAIL:工件校验失败,已中止(不排除网络篡改,可稍后重试或手动 update)"; rm -rf "$tmpd"; return 1
000850 |     fi
000851 | 
000852 |     # 跳过判断:剔除头部5个配置行后与本地已安装版本比对,无差异即已是最新
000853 |     strip_cfg() { grep -vE '^(PROXY_MODE|PROXY_GROUP|COUNTRY_TARGET|TZ_TARGET|LOCALE_TARGET)="' "$1"; }
000854 |     if [ -f "$BIN/claude-switch.sh" ] && \
000855 |        diff <(strip_cfg "$BIN/claude-switch.sh") <(strip_cfg "$NEW/core/claude-switch.sh") >/dev/null 2>&1; then
000856 |         echo "OK:已是最新版本"; rm -rf "$tmpd"; return 0
000857 |     fi
000858 | 
000859 |     # 备份现有核心脚本
000860 |     [ -f "$BIN/claude-switch.sh" ] && cp "$BIN/claude-switch.sh" "$BIN/claude-switch.sh.bak"
000861 | 
000862 |     # 替换核心脚本 + 从 .bak 读出5个配置值注入新脚本
000863 |     cp "$NEW/core/claude-switch.sh" "$BIN/claude-switch.sh"
000864 |     PM=""; PG=""; CT=""; TZV=""; LCT=""
000865 |     if [ -f "$BIN/claude-switch.sh.bak" ]; then
000866 |         get_cfg() { grep "^$1=\"" "$BIN/claude-switch.sh.bak" | head -1 | cut -d'"' -f2; }
000867 |         PM=$(get_cfg PROXY_MODE); PG=$(get_cfg PROXY_GROUP); CT=$(get_cfg COUNTRY_TARGET)
000868 |         TZV=$(get_cfg TZ_TARGET); LCT=$(get_cfg LOCALE_TARGET)
000869 |         inject_cfg() { grep -q "^$1=\"" "$BIN/claude-switch.sh.bak" && sed -i '' "s|^$1=\".*\"|$1=\"$2\"|" "$BIN/claude-switch.sh"; }
000870 |         inject_cfg PROXY_MODE "$PM"; inject_cfg PROXY_GROUP "$PG"; inject_cfg COUNTRY_TARGET "$CT"
000871 |         inject_cfg TZ_TARGET "$TZV"; inject_cfg LOCALE_TARGET "$LCT"
000872 |     fi
000873 |     chmod +x "$BIN/claude-switch.sh"
000874 | 
000875 |     # 守护进程脚本(root 所有,用户态不可写):与远程模板比对,有差异只提示不自动改
000876 |     APPDIR="/Library/Application Support/claude-switcher"
000877 |     if [ -f "$APPDIR/claude-bodyguard.sh" ] || [ -f "$APPDIR/tz-watchdog.sh" ]; then
000878 |         [ -z "$PM" ] && PM="clash-verge"
000879 |         [ -z "$TZV" ] && TZV="America/New_York"
000880 |         sed -e "s|__HOME__|$UHOME|g" -e "s|__PROXY_MODE__|$PM|g" "$NEW/daemons/claude-bodyguard.sh" > "$tmpd/bg.new"
000881 |         sed -e "s|__HOME__|$UHOME|g" -e "s|__TZ_TARGET__|$TZV|g" "$NEW/daemons/tz-watchdog.sh" > "$tmpd/tz.new"
000882 |         if ! diff -q "$tmpd/bg.new" "$APPDIR/claude-bodyguard.sh" >/dev/null 2>&1 || \
000883 |            ! diff -q "$tmpd/tz.new" "$APPDIR/tz-watchdog.sh" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
000884 |             echo "INFO:守护进程脚本有更新,请运行 installer/安装.command 刷新(需要密码)"
000885 |         fi
000886 |     fi
000887 |     rm -rf "$tmpd"
000888 | 
000889 |     # 更新后自检:root(旧版 LaunchDaemon)下切回真实用户执行,避免 defaults 读到 root 的配置
000890 |     if [ "$(id -u)" -eq 0 ]; then
000891 |         owner=$(stat -f '%Su' "$UHOME" 2>/dev/null)
000892 |         if [ -n "$owner" ]; then out=$(sudo -H -u "$owner" "$BIN/claude-switch.sh" checkdetail 2>&1); else out=""; fi
000893 |     else
000894 |         out=$("$BIN/claude-switch.sh" checkdetail 2>&1)
000895 |     fi
000896 |     if [ -z "$out" ] || ! validate_audit_output "$out" || echo "$out" | grep -q '=fail'; then
000897 |         [ -f "$BIN/claude-switch.sh.bak" ] && cp "$BIN/claude-switch.sh.bak" "$BIN/claude-switch.sh"
000898 |         echo "FAIL:新版自检未通过,已自动回滚"
000899 |         return 1
000900 |     fi
000901 |     echo "OK:更新完成 $(date '+%Y-%m-%d')"
000902 |     return 0
000903 | }

000905 | status() {
000906 |     cur="none"; [ -f "$BASE/current" ] && cur=$(cat "$BASE/current")
000907 |     hasA=0; [ -d "$BASE/accounts/A" ] && hasA=1
000908 |     hasB=0; [ -d "$BASE/accounts/B" ] && hasB=1
000909 |     echo "current=$cur hasA=$hasA hasB=$hasB"
000910 | }

000912 | quit_claude() {
000913 |     pgrep -f "/Applications/Claude.app/Contents/MacOS/Claude" >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
000914 |     osascript -e 'tell application "Claude" to quit' >/dev/null 2>&1
000915 |     for i in $(seq 1 15); do
000916 |         pgrep -f "/Applications/Claude.app/Contents/MacOS/Claude" >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
000917 |         sleep 1
000918 |     done
000919 |     return 1
000920 | }

000922 | archive_current() {
000923 |     cur="$1"
000924 |     rm -rf "$BASE/accounts/$cur.tmp"
000925 |     ditto "$DATA" "$BASE/accounts/$cur.tmp" && rm -rf "$BASE/accounts/$cur" && mv "$BASE/accounts/$cur.tmp" "$BASE/accounts/$cur"
000926 |     log "已存档账号$cur"
000927 | }
000928 | 
000929 | switch_to() {
000930 |     target="$1"
000931 |     case "$target" in
000932 |         A|B) ;;
000933 |         *) echo "FAIL:账号目标无效，只支持 A 或 B"; return 2 ;;
000934 |     esac
000935 |     launch_preflight || return 1
000936 | 
000937 |     cur="none"; [ -f "$BASE/current" ] && cur=$(cat "$BASE/current")
000938 | 
000939 |     # 首次运行:把当前登录中的状态收编为账号A
000940 |     if [ "$cur" = "none" ] && [ -d "$DATA" ] && [ -n "$(ls -A "$DATA" 2>/dev/null)" ]; then
000941 |         quit_claude || { echo "FAIL:Claude 未能退出，请手动退出后重试"; return 1; }
000942 |         archive_current A
000943 |         cur="A"; echo "$cur" > "$BASE/current"
000944 |         log "首次运行:当前登录状态已收编为账号A"
000945 |         echo "NOTE:已将当前登录收编为账号A"
000946 |     fi
000947 | 
000948 |     if [ "$cur" = "$target" ]; then
000949 |         open -a "Claude"
000950 |         echo "INFO:当前已是账号$target"
000951 |         return 0
000952 |     fi
000953 | 
000954 |     quit_claude || { echo "FAIL:Claude 未能退出，请手动退出后重试"; return 1; }
000955 | 
000956 |     [ "$cur" != "none" ] && [ -d "$DATA" ] && archive_current "$cur"
000957 | 
000958 |     rm -rf "$DATA.new"
000959 |     if [ -d "$BASE/accounts/$target" ]; then
000960 |         ditto "$BASE/accounts/$target" "$DATA.new"
000961 |         log "已载入账号$target 存档"
000962 |     else
000963 |         mkdir -p "$DATA.new"
000964 |         log "账号$target 无存档，将以全新状态初始化"
000965 |     fi
000966 |     rm -rf "$DATA" && mv "$DATA.new" "$DATA"
000967 |     echo "$target" > "$BASE/current"
000968 | 
000969 |     open -a "Claude"
000970 |     log "已切换到账号$target 并启动"
000971 |     echo "OK:已切换到账号$target"
000972 |     return 0
000973 | }
000974 | 
000975 | if [[ "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}" == "$0" ]]; then
000976 |     case "${1:-}" in
000977 |         check)  check ;;
000978 |         checkdetail)
000979 |             OUT_FILE="$BASE/last-check.txt"
000980 |             {
000981 |                 echo "# $(date '+%F %T') pid=$$ 开始"
000982 |                 checkdetail
000983 |                 echo "# 结束"
000984 |             } > "$OUT_FILE" 2>&1
000985 |             cat "$OUT_FILE"
000986 |             ;;
000987 |         fix) fix ;;
000988 |         status) status ;;
000989 |         switch) switch_to "${2:-}" ;;
000990 |         set-region) set_region ;;
000991 |         update) self_update "${2:-}" ;;
000992 |         write-merge-rules) write_merge_rules ;;  # 隐藏入口:供安装器调用,保证两处合并逻辑一致
000993 |         *) echo "用法: claude-switch.sh check|checkdetail|fix|status|switch A|B|set-region|update [--auto]|diagnose-proxy [--proxy URL|--config FILE]"; exit 2 ;;
000994 |     esac
000995 | fi


==============================================================================
FILE: current/core/stun-probe.py
SHA256: 2c891f5f3cf392a6bb736dacc65a945afe89ae8e4d88e353b04fd1f9cd792ab1
==============================================================================
000001 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
000002 | """WebRTC 泄漏探测:并发向多个 STUN 服务器(含国内节点)发 UDP Binding 请求。
000003 | 走隧道时应得到代理出口 IP,裸连时会暴露真实国内 IP。全程硬超时 10 秒,绝不阻塞。"""
000004 | import socket, struct, os, signal, threading
000005 | 
000006 | SERVERS = [
000007 |     ("stun.miwifi.com", 3478),        # 国内节点:裸连时必有响应
000008 |     ("stun.chat.bilibili.com", 3478), # 国内节点
000009 |     ("stun.l.google.com", 19302),     # 全球节点
000010 |     ("stun.cloudflare.com", 3478),    # 全球节点
000011 | ]
000012 | 
000013 | def stun_public_ip(host, port, timeout=2.0):
000014 |     try:
000015 |         addr = socket.gethostbyname(host)
000016 |     except Exception:
000017 |         return None
000018 |     txn = os.urandom(12)
000019 |     req = struct.pack("!HHI", 0x0001, 0, 0x2112A442) + txn
000020 |     s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
000021 |     s.settimeout(timeout)
000022 |     try:
000023 |         s.sendto(req, (addr, port))
000024 |         data, _ = s.recvfrom(1024)
000025 |     except Exception:
000026 |         return None
000027 |     finally:
000028 |         s.close()
000029 |     if len(data) < 20 or data[0:2] != b"\x01\x01":
000030 |         return None
000031 |     pos = 20
000032 |     while pos + 4 <= len(data):
000033 |         atype, alen = struct.unpack("!HH", data[pos:pos+4])
000034 |         aval = data[pos+4:pos+4+alen]
000035 |         if atype == 0x0020 and len(aval) >= 8 and aval[1] == 0x01:
000036 |             ip_int = struct.unpack("!I", aval[4:8])[0] ^ 0x2112A442
000037 |             return socket.inet_ntoa(struct.pack("!I", ip_int))
000038 |         if atype == 0x0001 and len(aval) >= 8 and aval[1] == 0x01:
000039 |             return socket.inet_ntoa(aval[4:8])
000040 |         pos += 4 + alen + ((4 - alen % 4) % 4)
000041 |     return None
000042 | 
000043 | def _alarm(signum, frame):
000044 |     raise TimeoutError
000045 | 
000046 | def main():
000047 |     found = []
000048 |     lock = threading.Lock()
000049 |     def worker(h, p):
000050 |         ip = stun_public_ip(h, p)
000051 |         if ip:
000052 |             with lock:
000053 |                 if ip not in found:
000054 |                     found.append(ip)
000055 |     threads = []
000056 |     for h, p in SERVERS:
000057 |         t = threading.Thread(target=worker, args=(h, p), daemon=True)
000058 |         t.start()
000059 |         threads.append(t)
000060 |     for t in threads:
000061 |         t.join(timeout=3)
000062 |     print("RESULT:" + (",".join(found) if found else "NONE"))
000063 | 
000064 | signal.signal(signal.SIGALRM, _alarm)
000065 | signal.alarm(10)
000066 | try:
000067 |     main()
000068 | except TimeoutError:
000069 |     print("RESULT:NONE")

FILE: current/daemons/claude-bodyguard.sh
SHA256: 5ff62d74ab764f40667ce83299e62cc139bb0392deef4235084f8b5b1523f74f

000001 | #!/bin/bash
000002 | # Claude 保镖 (参考模板) —— 安装程序会把 __HOME__ / __PROXY_MODE__ 替换为实际值后安装
000003 | # 作用: 代理(内核或TUN)离线时,自动关闭 Claude,杜绝裸连重试
000004 | MODE="__PROXY_MODE__"
000005 | 
000006 | ok=1
000007 | 
000008 | # TUN 在线判定:存在 fake-ip 网段地址即视为 TUN 已接管
000009 | ifconfig 2>/dev/null | grep -q 'inet 198\.18\.' || ok=0
000010 | 
000011 | # 内核在线判定:按安装时识别的客户端分派
000012 | case "$MODE" in
000013 |     clash-verge)
000014 |         pgrep -f verge-mihomo >/dev/null 2>&1 || ok=0 ;;
000015 |     shadowrocket)
000016 |         # launchd 环境下 scutil 可能查不到 VPN 行,查不到时以 TUN 判定为准(避免误杀)
000017 |         nc=$(scutil --nc list 2>/dev/null)
000018 |         if echo "$nc" | grep -q 'com.liguangming.Shadowrocket'; then
000019 |             echo "$nc" | grep 'com.liguangming.Shadowrocket' | grep -q '(Connected)' || ok=0
000020 |         fi ;;
000021 |     sing-box)
000022 |         pgrep -f sing-box >/dev/null 2>&1 || ok=0 ;;
000023 |     clashx)
000024 |         { pgrep -x ClashX >/dev/null 2>&1 || pgrep -f clash >/dev/null 2>&1; } || ok=0 ;;
000025 |     surge)
000026 |         pgrep -f Surge >/dev/null 2>&1 || ok=0 ;;
000027 |     auto)
000028 |         : ;;  # auto 模式只查 TUN
000029 |     *)
000030 |         pgrep -f verge-mihomo >/dev/null 2>&1 || ok=0 ;;
000031 | esac
000032 | 
000033 | if [ "$ok" -eq 0 ]; then
000034 |     if pgrep -f '/Applications/Claude.app/Contents/MacOS/Claude' >/dev/null 2>&1; then
000035 |         pkill -f '/Applications/Claude.app/Contents/MacOS/Claude'
000036 |         logger -t local.claude-bodyguard "代理离线(内核或TUN丢失),已自动关闭 Claude"
000037 |     fi
000038 | fi

000001 | #!/bin/bash
000002 | # 时区看门狗 (参考模板) —— 安装程序会把 __TZ_TARGET__ 和 __HOME__ 替换后安装
000003 | # 作用: 系统时区偏离目标值时自动改回,并记录日志
000004 | TARGET="__TZ_TARGET__"
000005 | 
000006 | current=$(readlink /etc/localtime 2>/dev/null)
000007 | if [ "$current" != "/var/db/timezone/zoneinfo/$TARGET" ]; then
000008 |     if /usr/sbin/systemsetup -settimezone "$TARGET"; then
000009 |         logger -t local.tz-watchdog "时区异常: [$current] 已恢复为 $TARGET"
000010 |     else
000011 |         logger -t local.tz-watchdog "时区恢复失败: [$current] 目标为 $TARGET"
000012 |         exit 1
000013 |     fi
000014 | fi

FILE: current/installer/安装.command
SHA256: d6d66d895a8240c2d600608d165e2a9e7bd3f6e0798380bef632450fb3a7f19c

000001 | #!/bin/bash
000002 | # Claude 切换器 - 通用安装程序 (macOS)
000003 | cd "$(dirname "$0")"
000004 | REPO="$(cd .. && pwd)"
000005 | H="$HOME"
000006 | clear
000007 | echo "=================================="
000008 | echo "  Claude 账号切换器 · 安装程序"
000009 | echo "=================================="
000010 | echo ""
000011 | 
000012 | # 国家代码 → 地区格式映射(未收录的国家回退 en_US)
000013 | locale_for() {
000014 |     case "$1" in
000015 |         US) echo en_US ;; JP) echo ja_JP ;; TW) echo zh_TW ;; HK) echo zh_HK ;;
000016 |         SG) echo en_SG ;; GB) echo en_GB ;; DE) echo de_DE ;; FR) echo fr_FR ;;
000017 |         KR) echo ko_KR ;; AU) echo en_AU ;; CA) echo en_CA ;; *) echo en_US ;;
000018 |     esac
000019 | }
000020 | 
000021 | # 国家与时区优先从 ipinfo 获取;遇到限流或无效响应时回退到 ipwho.is。
000022 | lookup_geo() {
000023 |     local url payload fields
000024 |     for url in https://ipinfo.io/json https://ipwho.is/ https://api.ip.sb/geoip; do
000025 |         payload=$(curl -fsS --max-time 8 "$url" 2>/dev/null) || continue
000026 |         fields=$(printf '%s' "$payload" | python3 -c '
000027 | import json, sys
000028 | try:
000029 |     data = json.load(sys.stdin)
000030 |     country = data.get("country_code") or data.get("country") or ""
000031 |     zone = data.get("timezone") or ""
000032 |     if isinstance(zone, dict):
000033 |         zone = zone.get("id", "")
000034 |     if len(country) == 2 and zone:
000035 |         print(country + "\t" + zone)
000036 | except Exception:
000037 |     pass
000038 | ' 2>/dev/null)
000039 |         if [ -n "$fields" ]; then printf '%s\n' "$fields"; return 0; fi
000040 |     done
000041 |     return 1
000042 | }
000043 | 
000044 | # ---- 0. 前置检查 ----
000045 | CLASH_DIR="$H/Library/Application Support/io.github.clash-verge-rev.clash-verge-rev"
000046 | if [ -d "$CLASH_DIR" ]; then
000047 |     MODE="clash-verge"
000048 | elif scutil --nc list 2>/dev/null | grep -q 'com.liguangming.Shadowrocket'; then
000049 |     MODE="shadowrocket"
000050 | elif pgrep -x 0dcloudCore >/dev/null 2>&1; then
000051 |     MODE="auto"
000052 | else
000053 |     echo "未自动识别到代理客户端,请手动选择:"
000054 |     echo "  1) clash-verge    2) shadowrocket    3) sing-box"
000055 |     echo "  4) clashx         5) surge           6) auto(任一命中即活)"
000056 |     read -r -p "请输入序号或名称: " pick
000057 |     case "$pick" in
000058 |         1|clash-verge)  MODE="clash-verge" ;;
000059 |         2|shadowrocket) MODE="shadowrocket" ;;
000060 |         3|sing-box)     MODE="sing-box" ;;
000061 |         4|clashx)       MODE="clashx" ;;
000062 |         5|surge)        MODE="surge" ;;
000063 |         6|auto)         MODE="auto" ;;
000064 |         *) echo "未识别的选择,退出"; exit 1 ;;
000065 |     esac
000066 | fi
000067 | echo "✓ 代理客户端: $MODE"
000068 | 
000069 | proxy_active() {
000070 |     case "$MODE" in
000071 |         clash-verge) pgrep -f verge-mihomo >/dev/null 2>&1 ;;
000072 |         shadowrocket) scutil --nc list 2>/dev/null | grep 'com.liguangming.Shadowrocket' | grep -q '(Connected)' ;;
000073 |         sing-box) pgrep -f sing-box >/dev/null 2>&1 ;;
000074 |         clashx) pgrep -x ClashX >/dev/null 2>&1 || pgrep -f clash >/dev/null 2>&1 ;;
000075 |         surge) pgrep -f Surge >/dev/null 2>&1 ;;
000076 |         auto)
000077 |             pgrep -f verge-mihomo >/dev/null 2>&1 || \
000078 |             scutil --nc list 2>/dev/null | grep 'com.liguangming.Shadowrocket' | grep -q '(Connected)' || \
000079 |             pgrep -f sing-box >/dev/null 2>&1 || pgrep -x 0dcloudCore >/dev/null 2>&1 || pgrep -x ClashX >/dev/null 2>&1 || \
000080 |             pgrep -f clash >/dev/null 2>&1 || pgrep -f Surge >/dev/null 2>&1 ;;
000081 |         *) return 1 ;;
000082 |     esac
000083 | }
000084 | 
000085 | if ! proxy_active; then
000086 |     echo "✗ $MODE 未运行或尚未连接。先启动代理客户端，再重新安装。"
000087 |     exit 1
000088 | fi
000089 | if ! ifconfig 2>/dev/null | grep -q 'inet 198\.18\.'; then
000090 |     echo "✗ 未检测到 TUN 网卡。先在代理客户端启用 TUN 并确认节点，再重新安装。"
000091 |     exit 1
000092 | fi
000093 | 
000094 | if [ ! -d /Applications/Claude.app ]; then
000095 |     echo "⚠️  未检测到 Claude 桌面版(/Applications/Claude.app),请先安装。"
000096 |     read -n 1 -s -r -p "按任意键退出..."; exit 1
000097 | fi
000098 | if ! python3 --version >/dev/null 2>&1; then
000099 |     echo "⚠️  未检测到 python3,请先安装 Xcode 命令行工具: xcode-select --install"
000100 |     read -n 1 -s -r -p "按任意键退出..."; exit 1
000101 | fi
000102 | if ! command -v swiftc >/dev/null 2>&1; then
000103 |     echo "⚠️  未检测到 swiftc(界面 App 需要现场编译),请先运行: xcode-select --install"
000104 |     read -n 1 -s -r -p "按任意键退出..."; exit 1
000105 | fi
000106 | case "$MODE" in
000107 |     clash-verge|shadowrocket|sing-box|clashx|surge|auto) ;;
000108 |     *) echo "✗ 未支持的代理客户端模式: $MODE"; exit 1 ;;
000109 | esac
000110 | echo "✓ 前置检查通过($MODE / Claude / python3 / swiftc)"
000111 | 
000112 | # ---- 1. 自动探测代理组名(仅 Clash Verge;多个 select 组时让用户选,避免静默选错专线) ----
000113 | GRP=""
000114 | if [ "$MODE" = "clash-verge" ]; then
000115 |     GRPS=()
000116 |     while IFS= read -r g; do GRPS+=("$g"); done < <(awk '
000117 |         /^proxy-groups:/ {f=1; next}
000118 |         f && /^[a-zA-Z]/ {f=0}
000119 |         f && /- name:/ { n=$0; sub(/.*- name: */, "", n); gsub(/"/, "", n) }
000120 |         f && /type: *select/ { print n }
000121 |     ' "$CLASH_DIR/clash-verge.yaml" 2>/dev/null)
000122 |     if [ "${#GRPS[@]}" -eq 0 ]; then
000123 |         echo ""
000124 |         echo "未能自动识别你的代理组名,请打开 Clash Verge 查看「代理」页面顶部的组名。"
000125 |         read -r -p "请输入代理组名: " GRP
000126 |         [ -z "$GRP" ] && { echo "未输入,退出"; exit 1; }
000127 |     elif [ "${#GRPS[@]}" -eq 1 ]; then
000128 |         GRP="${GRPS[0]}"
000129 |     else
000130 |         echo ""
000131 |         echo "检测到多个代理组,请选择用于 Claude 专线的那个:"
000132 |         i=1
000133 |         for g in "${GRPS[@]}"; do echo "  $i) $g"; i=$((i+1)); done
000134 |         read -r -p "请输入序号: " pick
000135 |         case "$pick" in ''|*[!0-9]*) echo "输入无效,退出"; exit 1 ;; esac
000136 |         if [ "$pick" -lt 1 ] || [ "$pick" -gt "${#GRPS[@]}" ]; then
000137 |             echo "序号超范围,退出"; exit 1
000138 |         fi
000139 |         GRP="${GRPS[$((pick-1))]}"
000140 |     fi
000141 |     echo "✓ 代理组: $GRP"
000142 | fi
000143 | if [[ "$GRP" =~ [^A-Za-z0-9_.[:space:]-] ]]; then
000144 |     echo "✗ 代理组名包含不支持的字符，拒绝写入安装配置。"
000145 |     exit 1
000146 | fi
000147 | 
000148 | # ---- 2. 地区自适应(按出口IP自动锁定 国家/时区/地区格式;必须先挂代理) ----
000149 | GEO=$(lookup_geo) || GEO=""
000150 | IFS=$'\t' read -r CTRY TZV GEO_IP <<< "$GEO"
000151 | if [ "$CTRY" = "CN" ]; then
000152 |     echo ""
000153 |     echo "❌ 检测到当前出口在国内(国家: CN)。"
000154 |     echo "   请先在代理客户端里开启代理(TUN模式)并确认节点可用,再运行本安装程序。"
000155 |     echo "   (否则时区/地区会被错误锁定)"
000156 |     exit 1
000157 | fi
000158 | if [ -z "$CTRY" ]; then
000159 |     echo "✗ 未能识别出口国家。请确认代理已连接后重试。"
000160 |     exit 1
000161 | fi
000162 | if [[ ! "$CTRY" =~ ^[A-Z]{2}$ ]] || [[ ! "$TZV" =~ ^[A-Za-z0-9_+-]+(/[A-Za-z0-9_+-]+)*$ ]] || [ ! -e "/usr/share/zoneinfo/$TZV" ]; then
000163 |     echo "✗ 出口国家或时区字段无效，拒绝继续安装。"
000164 |     exit 1
000165 | fi
000166 | LOCALE=$(locale_for "$CTRY")
000167 | echo "✓ 地区自适应: 国家=$CTRY 时区=$TZV 地区格式=$LOCALE (按当前出口IP自动识别)"
